   2  0000              ; Build NeoGS firmware and one deployable package from the Project 124 root:

# file opened: main.asm
   1+ 0000              ; =========================================================================
   2+ 0000              ; PROJECT 124 — NeoGS Z80 firmware / command dispatcher
   3+ 0000              ; Based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and ports.inc v0.3.
   4+ 0000              ;
   5+ 0000              ; MEMORY MAP / BOOT:
   6+ 0000              ; The stock GS105a ROM loads a tiny module at $5B00. That module enables
   7+ 0000              ; RAM-over-ROM and receives this firmware at logical RAM address $0000.
   8+ 0000              ; Current firmware image is kept below $3400; the stack grows down from $3FFF.
   9+ 0000              ; WIN1 remains on the documented default physical 16 KiB page #03.
  10+ 0000              ; The DMA frame at logical $5000 therefore maps to physical $00D000.
  11+ 0000              ; Firmware and its stack remain in the low 16 KiB page.
  12+ 0000              ; =========================================================================
  13+ 0000
  14+ 0000                      INCLUDE "../shared/neogs_io.inc"
# file opened: neogs_io.inc
   1++0000              ; =========================================================================
   2++0000              ; NeoGS ports.inc v0.3 — Project 124 used subset
   3++0000              ; Hardware port names/addresses from NedoPC ports.inc v0.3.
   4++0000              ; =========================================================================
   5++0000
   6++0000              ; ZX Spectrum side
   7++0000              GSCOM           EQU #BB             ; write command / read status
   8++0000              GSSTAT          EQU #BB             ; read CBIT (bit 0), DBIT (bit 7)
   9++0000              GSDAT           EQU #B3             ; read/write data byte
  10++0000              GSCTR           EQU #33             ; global control
  11++0000
  12++0000              B_CBIT          EQU 0
  13++0000              M_CBIT          EQU #01
  14++0000              B_DBIT          EQU 7
  15++0000              M_DBIT          EQU #80
  16++0000
  17++0000              C_GRST          EQU #80
  18++0000              C_GNMI          EQU #40
  19++0000              C_GLED          EQU #20
  20++0000
  21++0000              ; NeoGS Z80 local ports
  22++0000              MPAG            EQU #00
  23++0000              MPAGEX          EQU #10             ; independent 16 KiB page for C000-FFFF
  24++0000              ZXCMD           EQU #01
  25++0000              ZXDATRD         EQU #02
  26++0000              ZXDATWR         EQU #03
  27++0000              ZXSTAT          EQU #04
  28++0000              CLRCBIT         EQU #05
  29++0000

  31++0000              B_NOROM         EQU 0
  32++0000              M_NOROM         EQU #01
  33++0000              B_RAMRO         EQU 1
  34++0000              M_RAMRO         EQU #02
  35++0000              B_8CHANS        EQU 2
  36++0000              M_8CHANS        EQU #04
  37++0000              B_EXPAG         EQU 3
  38++0000              M_EXPAG         EQU #08
  39++0000              B_CKSEL0        EQU 4
  40++0000              M_CKSEL0        EQU #10
  41++0000              B_CKSEL1        EQU 5
  42++0000              M_CKSEL1        EQU #20
  43++0000              C_10MHZ         EQU #30
  44++0000              C_12MHZ         EQU #10

  46++0000              C_24MHZ         EQU #00
  47++0000              B_PAN4CH        EQU 6
  48++0000              M_PAN4CH        EQU #40
  49++0000              B_INV7B         EQU 7
  50++0000              M_INV7B         EQU #80
  51++0000

  53++0000              PG0             EQU #20
  54++0000              PG1             EQU #21
  55++0000              PG2             EQU #22
  56++0000              PG3             EQU #23
  57++0000

  59++0000              DMA_MOD         EQU #1B

  62++0000              DMA_LAD         EQU #1E
  63++0000              DMA_CST         EQU #1F
  64++0000              C_DMA_NONE      EQU 0
  65++0000              C_DMA_ZX        EQU 1
  66++0000              C_DMA_SD        EQU 2
  67++0000              C_DMA_MP3       EQU 3
  68++0000
  69++0000              ; Standard General Sound ROM commands.
  70++0000              GS_CMD_RESET_FLAGS  EQU #00
  71++0000              GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM EQU #20
  72++0000              GS_CMD_LOAD_MODULE  EQU #30
  73++0000              GS_CMD_RUN_MODULE   EQU #31
  74++0000              GS_CMD_OPEN_STREAM  EQU #D1
  75++0000              GS_CMD_CLOSE_STREAM EQU #D2
  76++0000              GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART EQU #F4
  77++0000              GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS EQU #13
  78++0000              GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK EQU #14
  79++0000
  80++0000              ; Project 124 private commands carried by GSCOM after custom firmware starts.
  81++0000              CMD_RESET       EQU #01
  82++0000              CMD_LOAD_X86    EQU #02
  83++0000              CMD_RUN_X86     EQU #03
  84++0000              CMD_GET_STATUS  EQU #04
  85++0000              CMD_MEM_TEST    EQU #05
  86++0000              CMD_BIOS_POST   EQU #07
  87++0000              CMD_INT_TEST    EQU #08
  88++0000              CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST EQU #09
  89++0000              CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST EQU #0A
  90++0000              CMD_RUN_X86_REAL EQU #0B
  91++0000

  93++0000              NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK EQU #A1
  94++0000              NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK EQU #A3

  96++0000              NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK EQU #E1
  97++0000              NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE   EQU #A5
  98++0000
  99++0000              NGS_STATUS_CBIT EQU M_CBIT
 100++0000              NGS_STATUS_DBIT EQU M_DBIT
 101++0000
 102++0000              ; The proven x86 DMA frame occupies logical $5000..$7FFF (12 KiB).
 103++0000              ; Default NeoGS WIN1 page #03 maps logical $5000 to physical $00D000.
 104++0000              ; The two-byte length header leaves #2FFE bytes for the guest image.
 105++0000              NGS_X86_DMA_WINDOW_SIZE EQU #3000
 106++0000              NGS_X86_MAX_CODE_SIZE   EQU #2FFE

  16+ 0000                      ORG     $0000
  17+ 0000
  18+ 0000              ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR EQU $4000
  19+ 0000              X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR        EQU $5000
  20+ 0000              X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR           EQU $5002
  21+ 0000              DMA_WAIT_LIMIT   EQU #FFFF
  22+ 0000              DMA_WAIT_OUTER   EQU 8

  25+ 0000              ; Firmware entry vector
  26+ 0000 C3 00 01             jp      start_neoprogram
  27+ 0003 00 00 00...          BLOCK   53, 0
  28+ 0038 FB                   ei
  29+ 0039 C9                   ret
  30+ 003A 00 00 00...          BLOCK   198, 0
  31+ 0100
  32+ 0100              start_neoprogram:
  33+ 0100 F3                   di
  34+ 0101 ED 56                im      1
  35+ 0103 31 FE FF             ld      sp, $FFFE
  36+ 0106
  37+ 0106                      ; Set Z80 to the documented maximum guaranteed clock: 20 MHz.
  38+ 0106                      ; Do not select 24 MHz: the guide explicitly says it is not guaranteed.
  39+ 0106 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  40+ 0108 E6 CF                and     #CF
  41+ 010A F6 20                or      C_20MHZ
  42+ 010C D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  43+ 010E
  44+ 010E AF                   xor     a
  45+ 010F 32 FB 03             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
  46+ 0112 32 FC 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), a
  47+ 0115 32 FD 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax+1), a
  48+ 0118 32 FE 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), a
  49+ 011B 32 FF 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax+1), a
  50+ 011E 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
  51+ 0121 32 F9 03             ld      (neo_command), a
  52+ 0124 32 FA 03             ld      (neo_tx_byte), a
  53+ 0127
  54+ 0127                      ; Copy the ROM-return trampoline into unoverlaid RAM at $4000.
  55+ 0127 21 00 04             ld      hl, rom_return_trampoline_code
  56+ 012A 11 00 40             ld      de, ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR
  57+ 012D 01 0C 00             ld      bc, rom_return_trampoline_end - rom_return_trampoline_code
  58+ 0130 ED B0                ldir
  59+ 0132
  60+ 0132                      ; Notify the Spectrum only after the new firmware is executing.
  61+ 0132 3E A2                ld      a, NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
  62+ 0134 CD CC 03             call    neo_send_data_byte
  63+ 0137
  64+ 0137              main_loop:
  65+ 0137                      ; A command exists only while CBIT (ZXSTAT bit 0) is set.
  66+ 0137 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
  67+ 0139 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
  68+ 013B 28 FA                jr      z, main_loop
  69+ 013D
  70+ 013D                      ; Read the command byte from ZXCMD. Reading it does NOT clear CBIT.
  71+ 013D DB 01                in      a, (ZXCMD)
  72+ 013F 32 F9 03             ld      (neo_command), a
  73+ 0142
  74+ 0142                      ; Standard GS #F4 cold restart also returns this custom firmware to ROM.
  75+ 0142 FE F4                cp      GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
  76+ 0144 CA 7F 01             jp      z, command_return_to_gsrom
  77+ 0147 FE 01                cp      CMD_RESET
  78+ 0149 CA 87 01             jp      z, command_reset
  79+ 014C FE 02                cp      CMD_LOAD_X86
  80+ 014E CA A4 01             jp      z, command_load_x86
  81+ 0151 FE 03                cp      CMD_RUN_X86
  82+ 0153 CA F4 01             jp      z, command_run_x86
  83+ 0156 FE 04                cp      CMD_GET_STATUS
  84+ 0158 CA 46 03             jp      z, command_get_status
  85+ 015B FE 05                cp      CMD_MEM_TEST
  86+ 015D CA 0B 03             jp      z, command_mem_test
  87+ 0160 FE 07                cp      CMD_BIOS_POST
  88+ 0162 CA 14 03             jp      z, command_bios_post
  89+ 0165 FE 08                cp      CMD_INT_TEST
  90+ 0167 CA 1D 03             jp      z, command_int_test
  91+ 016A FE 09                cp      CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
  92+ 016C CA 26 03             jp      z, command_int_guest_test
  93+ 016F FE 0A                cp      CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
  94+ 0171 CA 2F 03             jp      z, command_bios_video_test
  95+ 0174 FE 0B                cp      CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
  96+ 0176 CA 38 03             jp      z, command_run_x86_real
  97+ 0179
  98+ 0179                      ; Unknown command: release CBIT so the host cannot deadlock.
  99+ 0179 AF                   xor     a
 100+ 017A D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 101+ 017C C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 102+ 017F
 103+ 017F              ; A #F4 command is also the standard GS105a cold-restart command.
 104+ 017F              ; If our firmware is running, reveal the stock ROM again and jump to its
 105+ 017F              ; reset vector. Leave CBIT asserted: the ROM's cold-start path clears it when
 106+ 017F              ; the standard firmware is ready, so the host's long wait is a real handshake.
 107+ 017F              command_return_to_gsrom:
 108+ 017F 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 109+ 0181 CD F6 03             call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore trampoline's physical page
 110+ 0184 C3 00 40             jp      ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR
 111+ 0187
 112+ 0187              command_reset:
 113+ 0187 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 114+ 0189 CD F6 03             call    neo_set_pg1             ; ensure firmware page 1 is visible
 115+ 018C AF                   xor     a
 116+ 018D 32 FB 03             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 117+ 0190 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 118+ 0193 32 FC 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), a
 119+ 0196 32 FD 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax+1), a
 120+ 0199 32 FE 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), a
 121+ 019C 32 FF 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax+1), a
 122+ 019F D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 123+ 01A1 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 124+ 01A4
 125+ 01A4              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 126+ 01A4              ; CMD_LOAD_X86
 127+ 01A4              ; 1) Keep default WIN1 page #03 selected; logical $5000 maps to $00D000.
 128+ 01A4              ; 2) Select ZX DMA module and set physical DMA target $00D000.
 129+ 01A4              ; 3) Enable DMA by setting DMA_CST bit 7.
 130+ 01A4              ; 4) Clear CBIT: this is the READY indication to the host.
 131+ 01A4              ; 5) Wait for host's end marker (#FF written to GSCOM, setting CBIT).
 132+ 01A4              ; 6) Disable DMA, then clear CBIT to acknowledge completion.
 133+ 01A4              ;
 134+ 01A4              ; Host uses OUT/LDIR writes into $0000-$3FFF. No dummy write is performed.
 135+ 01A4              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 136+ 01A4              command_load_x86:
 137+ 01A4                      ; Keep the documented default WIN1 page selected. DMA writes to
 138+ 01A4                      ; physical $00D000, which appears at logical $5000 in page #03.
 139+ 01A4 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 140+ 01A6 CD F6 03             call    neo_set_pg1
 141+ 01A9
 142+ 01A9 3E 01                ld      a, C_DMA_ZX
 143+ 01AB D3 1B                out     (DMA_MOD), a
 144+ 01AD
 145+ 01AD AF                   xor     a
 146+ 01AE D3 1C                out     (DMA_HAD), a            ; physical address bits 21:16 = 0
 147+ 01B0 3E D0                ld      a, #D0
 148+ 01B2 D3 1D                out     (DMA_MAD), a            ; physical address bits 15:8 = #D0
 149+ 01B4 AF                   xor     a
 150+ 01B5 D3 1E                out     (DMA_LAD), a            ; physical address bits 7:0 = 0
 151+ 01B7
 152+ 01B7 3E 80                ld      a, #80
 153+ 01B9 D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a            ; DMA enable = bit 7
 154+ 01BB
 155+ 01BB AF                   xor     a
 156+ 01BC D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a            ; host may now start LDIR
 157+ 01BE
 158+ 01BE 16 08                ld      d, DMA_WAIT_OUTER
 159+ 01C0              .dma_wait_outer:
 160+ 01C0 01 FF FF             ld      bc, DMA_WAIT_LIMIT
 161+ 01C3              .dma_wait_host_end:
 162+ 01C3 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 163+ 01C5 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
 164+ 01C7 20 1F                jr      nz, .dma_host_end
 165+ 01C9 0B                   dec     bc
 166+ 01CA 78                   ld      a, b
 167+ 01CB B1                   or      c
 168+ 01CC 20 F5                jr      nz, .dma_wait_host_end
 169+ 01CE 15                   dec     d
 170+ 01CF 20 EF                jr      nz, .dma_wait_outer
 171+ 01D1
 172+ 01D1                      ; Timeout: close DMA, restore PG1 and release the host command line.
 173+ 01D1 AF                   xor     a
 174+ 01D2 D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a
 175+ 01D4 D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 176+ 01D6 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 177+ 01D8 CD F6 03             call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore page 1 on DMA timeout
 178+ 01DB 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 179+ 01DD 32 FB 03             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 180+ 01E0 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 181+ 01E2 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 182+ 01E5 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 183+ 01E8
 184+ 01E8              .dma_host_end:
 185+ 01E8 AF                   xor     a
 186+ 01E9 D3 1F                out     (DMA_CST), a            ; disable DMA before leaving handler
 187+ 01EB D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a            ; acknowledge host's #FF marker
 188+ 01ED AF                   xor     a
 189+ 01EE 32 FB 03             ld      (neo_dma_error), a
 190+ 01F1 C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 191+ 01F4              neogs_dma_handler_end:
 192+ 01F4
 193+ 01F4              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 194+ 01F4              ; CMD_RUN_X86 — execute the length-framed payload beginning at $5002.
 195+ 01F4              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 196+ 01F4              command_run_x86:
 197+ 01F4 CD 02 02             call    execute_neogs_x86
 198+ 01F7 3E 03                ld      a, #03
 199+ 01F9 CD F6 03             call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore firmware/trampoline page
 200+ 01FC AF                   xor     a
 201+ 01FD D3 05                out     (CLRCBIT), a
 202+ 01FF C3 37 01             jp      main_loop
 203+ 0202
 204+ 0202              execute_neogs_x86:
 205+ 0202 3A FB 03             ld      a, (neo_dma_error)
 206+ 0205 B7                   or      a
 207+ 0206 28 0C                jr      z, .dma_ok
 208+ 0208 21 FF FF             ld      hl, $FFFF
 209+ 020B 22 FE 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 210+ 020E 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 211+ 0210 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 212+ 0213 C9                   ret
 213+ 0214
 214+ 0214              .dma_ok:
 215+ 0214                      ; Emit a live NeoGS-side dump before interpreting the DMA payload.
 216+ 0214                      ; The host drains this stream and mirrors it to emulator port #EF.
 217+ 0214 CD 70 03             call    neo_debug_dump_dma_frame
 218+ 0217
 219+ 0217                      ; DMA frame begins with a little-endian x86 byte length at $5000.
 220+ 0217 2A 00 50             ld      hl, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 221+ 021A 7C                   ld      a, h
 222+ 021B B5                   or      l
 223+ 021C CA 51 02             jp      z, .invalid_x86_length
 224+ 021F
 225+ 021F                      ; Maximum code length is $2FFE: $5002 + length must not exceed $8000.
 226+ 021F 7C                   ld      a, h
 227+ 0220 FE 2F                cp      #2F
 228+ 0222 38 09                jr      c, .x86_length_valid
 229+ 0224 C2 51 02             jp      nz, .invalid_x86_length
 230+ 0227 7D                   ld      a, l
 231+ 0228 FE FF                cp      #FF
 232+ 022A D2 51 02             jp      nc, .invalid_x86_length
 233+ 022D
 234+ 022D              .x86_length_valid:
 235+ 022D 11 02 50             ld      de, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 236+ 0230 19                   add     hl, de
 237+ 0231 22 02 0D             ld      (x86_program_end), hl
 238+ 0234
 239+ 0234 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
 240+ 0237 22 FC 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
 241+ 023A 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 242+ 023D 22 00 0D             ld      (x86_steps_remaining), hl
 243+ 0240 AF                   xor     a
 244+ 0241 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 245+ 0244
 246+ 0244 01 02 50             ld      bc, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 247+ 0247 CD 17 0A             call    x86_execute_loop
 248+ 024A
 249+ 024A 2A FC 03             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
 250+ 024D 22 FE 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 251+ 0250 C9                   ret
 252+ 0251
 253+ 0251              .invalid_x86_length:
 254+ 0251 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 255+ 0254 22 FC 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
 256+ 0257 22 FE 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 257+ 025A 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 258+ 025C 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 259+ 025F C9                   ret
 260+ 0260
 261+ 0260              ; Execute the loaded DMA task as bounded guest code at 0000:5002.
 262+ 0260              ; The DMA frame is copied into the x86 physical-memory backend before execution.
 263+ 0260              execute_neogs_x86_real:
 264+ 0260                      ; Match the legacy loader's DMA error and length validation.
 265+ 0260 3A FB 03             ld      a, (neo_dma_error)
 266+ 0263 B7                   or      a
 267+ 0264 28 0D                jr      z, .real_dma_ok
 268+ 0266 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 269+ 0269 22 FE 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 270+ 026C 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 271+ 026E 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 272+ 0271 37                   scf
 273+ 0272 C9                   ret
 274+ 0273
 275+ 0273              .real_dma_ok:
 276+ 0273 2A 00 50             ld      hl, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 277+ 0276 7C                   ld      a, h
 278+ 0277 B5                   or      l
 279+ 0278 CA F1 02             jp      z, .real_invalid_length
 280+ 027B 7C                   ld      a, h
 281+ 027C FE 2F                cp      #2F
 282+ 027E 38 09                jr      c, .real_length_valid
 283+ 0280 C2 F1 02             jp      nz, .real_invalid_length
 284+ 0283 7D                   ld      a, l
 285+ 0284 FE FF                cp      #FF
 286+ 0286 D2 F1 02             jp      nc, .real_invalid_length
 287+ 0289
 288+ 0289              .real_length_valid:
 289+ 0289 11 02 50             ld      de, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 290+ 028C 19                   add     hl, de
 291+ 028D 22 BB 17             ld      (x86_real_task_end), hl
 292+ 0290
 293+ 0290 CD 0C 04             call    x86_mem_init
 294+ 0293 21 02 50             ld      hl, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
 295+ 0296 11 02 50             ld      de, #5002
 296+ 0299 ED 4B 00 50          ld      bc, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
 297+ 029D CD 6F 10             call    x86_real_copy_image
 298+ 02A0 DA FE 02             jp      c, .real_copy_failed
 299+ 02A3
 300+ 02A3                      ; Reset the guest register file to a deterministic real-mode entry.
 301+ 02A3 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
 302+ 02A6 22 F1 09             ld      (x86_reg_ax), hl
 303+ 02A9 22 F7 09             ld      (x86_reg_bx), hl
 304+ 02AC 22 F3 09             ld      (x86_reg_cx), hl
 305+ 02AF 22 F5 09             ld      (x86_reg_dx), hl
 306+ 02B2 22 FB 09             ld      (x86_reg_bp), hl
 307+ 02B5 22 FD 09             ld      (x86_reg_si), hl
 308+ 02B8 22 FF 09             ld      (x86_reg_di), hl
 309+ 02BB 22 01 0A             ld      (x86_reg_es), hl
 310+ 02BE 22 07 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ds), hl
 311+ 02C1 22 03 0A             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
 312+ 02C4 21 02 50             ld      hl, #5002
 313+ 02C7 22 09 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
 314+ 02CA 21 00 70             ld      hl, #7000
 315+ 02CD 22 05 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ss), hl
 316+ 02D0 21 00 01             ld      hl, #0100
 317+ 02D3 22 F9 09             ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
 318+ 02D6 21 02 02             ld      hl, #0202
 319+ 02D9 22 0B 0A             ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl
 320+ 02DC
 321+ 02DC 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 322+ 02DE 32 BA 17             ld      (x86_real_bounds_enable), a
 323+ 02E1 CD FE 10             call    x86_real_execute
 324+ 02E4 F5                   push    af
 325+ 02E5 2A F1 09             ld      hl, (x86_reg_ax)
 326+ 02E8 22 FE 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 327+ 02EB AF                   xor     a
 328+ 02EC 32 BA 17             ld      (x86_real_bounds_enable), a
 329+ 02EF F1                   pop     af
 330+ 02F0 C9                   ret
 331+ 02F1
 332+ 02F1              .real_invalid_length:
 333+ 02F1 21 FF FF             ld      hl, #FFFF
 334+ 02F4 22 FE 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 335+ 02F7 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 336+ 02F9 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 337+ 02FC 37                   scf
 338+ 02FD C9                   ret
 339+ 02FE
 340+ 02FE              .real_copy_failed:
 341+ 02FE 21 01 1D             ld      hl, #1D01
 342+ 0301 22 FE 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 343+ 0304 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 344+ 0306 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 345+ 0309 37                   scf
 346+ 030A C9                   ret
 347+ 030B
 348+ 030B              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 349+ 030B              ; CMD_MEM_TEST — destructive test of the reserved 1 MiB x86 RAM region.
 350+ 030B              ; Result is returned through the existing CMD_GET_STATUS frame.

 489+ 03CC              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 490+ 03CC              ; neo_send_data_byte
 491+ 03CC              ; IN: A = byte to send to Spectrum.
 492+ 03CC              ; Wait for DBIT=0, write ZXDATWR (#03), then wait for host read to clear DBIT.
 493+ 03CC              ; OUT: carry clear on success, carry set on timeout. Preserves HL and DE.
 494+ 03CC              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 495+ 03CC              neo_send_data_byte:
 496+ 03CC 32 FA 03             ld      (neo_tx_byte), a
 497+ 03CF 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_WAIT_LIMIT
 498+ 03D2              .tx_wait_slot:

 500+ 03D4 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 501+ 03D6 28 07                jr      z, .tx_slot_free
 502+ 03D8 0B                   dec     bc
 503+ 03D9 78                   ld      a, b
 504+ 03DA B1                   or      c
 505+ 03DB 20 F5                jr      nz, .tx_wait_slot
 506+ 03DD 37                   scf
 507+ 03DE C9                   ret
 508+ 03DF
 509+ 03DF              .tx_slot_free:
 510+ 03DF 3A FA 03             ld      a, (neo_tx_byte)
 511+ 03E2 D3 03                out     (ZXDATWR), a
 512+ 03E4
 513+ 03E4 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_WAIT_LIMIT
 514+ 03E7              .tx_wait_host_read:
 515+ 03E7 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 516+ 03E9 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 517+ 03EB 28 07                jr      z, .tx_host_read
 518+ 03ED 0B                   dec     bc
 519+ 03EE 78                   ld      a, b
 520+ 03EF B1                   or      c
 521+ 03F0 20 F5                jr      nz, .tx_wait_host_read
 522+ 03F2 37                   scf
 523+ 03F3 C9                   ret
 524+ 03F4
 525+ 03F4              .tx_host_read:

 527+ 03F5 C9                   ret

 530+ 03F6              ; Select the physical 16 KiB page visible in NeoGS logical window 1.
 531+ 03F6              ; WIN1 is the GS-side port #21 from the official ports.inc interface.
 532+ 03F6              ; Input: A = physical 16 KiB page number.
 533+ 03F6              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------

 544+ 0400              ; This code is copied to $4000 during startup. Its OUT changes the ROM map,
 545+ 0400              ; but the following JP is still fetched from RAM because PC remains in $4000.
 546+ 0400              rom_return_trampoline_code:
 547+ 0400 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
 548+ 0402 E6 F6                and     #F6                    ; clear M_NOROM and M_EXPAG

# file opened: x86_memory.asm
   1++040C              ; =========================================================================
   2++040C              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 physical memory backend for NeoGS GS105a
   3++040C              ;
   4++040C              ; x86 physical $00000-$FFFFF maps to NeoGS RAM pages 4..67
   5++040C              ; (NeoGS physical base $010000, page size $4000 / 16 KiB).
   6++040C              ; Window C000-FFFF is switched through MPAGEX. This keeps firmware in
   7++040C              ; window 0 / page 0 and the current DMA frame in window 1 / page 3.
   8++040C              ;
   9++040C              ; x86_mem_addr is a 20-bit little-endian address:
  10++040C              ;   +0 = bits 7:0, +1 = bits 15:8, +2 = bits 19:16 (valid 0..0F).
  11++040C              ;
  12++040C              ; API:
  13++040C              ;   x86_mem_init: enable NOROM + EXPAG, preserving other GSCFG0 bits.
  14++040C              ;   x86_mem_read8:  IN x86_mem_addr; OUT A=value, carry=invalid address.
  15++040C              ;   x86_mem_write8: IN x86_mem_addr and x86_mem_value; carry=invalid address.
  16++040C              ;   x86_mem_test: destructive test of the reserved 1 MiB x86 RAM region.
  17++040C              ;                 status=0, AX=0001 on pass; status=5, AX=phase:page on fail.
  18++040C              ;
  19++040C              ; The test leaves NOROM+EXPAG enabled. Only window 3 is switched by MPAGEX.
  20++040C              ; =========================================================================
  21++040C
  22++040C              X86_MEM_NGSPAGE_BASE EQU 4
  23++040C              X86_MEM_PAGE_COUNT   EQU 64
  24++040C
  25++040C              x86_mem_init:
  26++040C DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  27++040E F6 09                or      M_NOROM | M_EXPAG
  28++0410 D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  29++0412 C9                   ret
  30++0413
  31++0413              ; Convert x86_mem_addr into a Z80 pointer in C000-FFFF.
  32++0413              ; OUT: HL=window pointer, carry clear; carry set if address > FFFFF.
  33++0413              x86_mem_map_addr:
  34++0413 3A 5B 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+2)
  35++0416 FE 10                cp      #10
  36++0418 30 1E                jr      nc, .bad_address
  37++041A
  38++041A                      ; page = (physical >> 14) + 4
  39++041A                      ; page index = (high_nibble << 2) | (middle_byte >> 6)
  40++041A 87                   add     a, a
  41++041B 87                   add     a, a
  42++041C 47                   ld      b, a
  43++041D 3A 5A 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+1)
  44++0420 07                   rlca
  45++0421 07                   rlca
  46++0422 E6 03                and     #03
  47++0424 B0                   or      b
  48++0425 C6 04                add     a, X86_MEM_NGSPAGE_BASE
  49++0427
  50++0427                      ; MPAGEX uses ROL8(page,1); send ROR8(page,1).
  51++0427 0F                   rrca
  52++0428 D3 10                out     (MPAGEX), a
  53++042A
  54++042A                      ; Window C000-FFFF maps the selected 16 KiB physical page.
  55++042A 3A 5A 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr+1)
  56++042D E6 3F                and     #3F
  57++042F F6 C0                or      #C0
  58++0431 67                   ld      h, a
  59++0432 3A 59 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_addr)
  60++0435 6F                   ld      l, a
  61++0436 B7                   or      a
  62++0437 C9                   ret
  63++0438
  64++0438              .bad_address:
  65++0438 37                   scf
  66++0439 C9                   ret
  67++043A
  68++043A              ; Read one byte. IN x86_mem_addr[0..2]. OUT A=byte, carry on invalid address.
  69++043A              x86_mem_read8:
  70++043A CD 13 04             call    x86_mem_map_addr
  71++043D D8                   ret     c
  72++043E 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
  73++043F B7                   or      a
  74++0440 C9                   ret
  75++0441
  76++0441              ; Write one byte. IN x86_mem_addr[0..2], x86_mem_value.
  77++0441              x86_mem_write8:
  78++0441 CD 13 04             call    x86_mem_map_addr
  79++0444 D8                   ret     c
  80++0445 3A 5C 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_value)
  81++0448 77                   ld      (hl), a
  82++0449 B7                   or      a
  83++044A C9                   ret
  84++044B
  85++044B              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  86++044B              ; Destructive physical RAM mapping test.
  87++044B              ; Every page receives unique values at offsets 0000 and 3FFF. A second pass
  88++044B              ; verifies all 128 locations, detecting missing pages and page aliases.
  89++044B              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  90++044B              x86_mem_test:
  91++044B CD 0C 04             call    x86_mem_init
  92++044E AF                   xor     a
  93++044F 32 5D 06             ld      (x86_mem_page), a
  94++0452 32 5E 06             ld      (x86_mem_phase), a
  95++0455
  96++0455              .write_page_loop:
  97++0455 CD 1E 06             call    x86_mem_build_page_start
  98++0458 3A 5D 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_page)
  99++045B EE 5A                xor     #5A
 100++045D 32 5C 06             ld      (x86_mem_value), a
 101++0460 CD 41 04             call    x86_mem_write8
 102++0463 DA FC 05             jp      c, x86_mem_test_write_start_fail
 103++0466
 104++0466 CD 3A 06             call    x86_mem_build_page_end
 105++0469 3A 5D 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_page)
 106++046C EE A5                xor     #A5
 107++046E 32 5C 06             ld      (x86_mem_value), a
 108++0471 CD 41 04             call    x86_mem_write8
 109++0474 DA 00 06             jp      c, x86_mem_test_write_end_fail
 110++0477
 111++0477 3A 5D 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_page)
 112++047A 3C                   inc     a
 113++047B 32 5D 06             ld      (x86_mem_page), a
 114++047E FE 40                cp      X86_MEM_PAGE_COUNT
 115++0480 38 D3                jr      c, .write_page_loop
 116++0482

 150++04BB                      ; Success: AX=0001 means all memory/addressing tests passed.
 151++04BB 21 01 00             ld      hl, 1
 152++04BE 22 FE 03             ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
 153++04C1 AF                   xor     a
 154++04C2 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
 155++04C5 C9                   ret
 156++04C6
 157++04C6              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 158++04C6              ; Segment:offset -> physical address.
 159++04C6              ; Uses x86_mem_segment and x86_mem_offset_work; result in x86_mem_addr.
 160++04C6              ; Physical address = ((segment << 4) + offset) & FFFFFh.
 161++04C6              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 162++04C6              x86_mem_calc_phys:
 163++04C6                      ; Byte 0 = (segment low << 4) + offset low.
 164++04C6 3A 5F 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_segment)
 165++04C9 07                   rlca
 166++04CA 07                   rlca
 167++04CB 07                   rlca
 168++04CC 07                   rlca
 169++04CD 47                   ld      b, a
 170++04CE 3A 63 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_offset_work)
 171++04D1 80                   add     a, b
 172++04D2 32 59 06             ld      (x86_mem_addr), a
 173++04D5 3E 00                ld      a, 0
 174++04D7 CE 00                adc     a, 0
 175++04D9 32 67 06             ld      (x86_mem_carry), a
 176++04DC
 177++04DC                      ; Byte 1 base = (segment low >> 4) | (segment high << 4).
 178++04DC 3A 5F 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_segment)
 179++04DF 0F                   rrca
 180++04E0 0F                   rrca
 181++04E1 0F                   rrca
 182++04E2 0F                   rrca

 186++04E9 07                   rlca
 187++04EA 07                   rlca
 188++04EB 07                   rlca
 189++04EC 07                   rlca
 190++04ED B0                   or      b
 191++04EE
 192++04EE                      ; Add offset high to byte-1 base, then add byte-0 carry.
 193++04EE                      ; The two carry-outs are mutually exclusive for byte addition.
 194++04EE 47                   ld      b, a
 195++04EF 3A 64 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_offset_work+1)
 196++04F2 80                   add     a, b
 197++04F3 57                   ld      d, a
 198++04F4 3E 00                ld      a, 0
 199++04F6 CE 00                adc     a, 0
 200++04F8 32 68 06             ld      (x86_mem_carry1), a   ; carry from base + offset high
 201++04FB
 202++04FB 7A                   ld      a, d
 203++04FC 47                   ld      b, a
 204++04FD 3A 67 06             ld      a, (x86_mem_carry)
 205++0500 80                   add     a, b
 206++0501 32 5A 06             ld      (x86_mem_addr+1), a

 217++0514 0F                   rrca

 228++0524              ; Read a little-endian word at segment:offset.
 229++0524              ; IN: x86_mem_segment, x86_mem_offset. OUT: HL=word, carry=invalid.

 231++0524              x86_mem_read16:

 269++056A C9                   ret

   1++0669              ; =========================================================================
   2++0669              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS foundation / POST, stage 1
   3++0669              ;
   4++0669              ; Establishes the guest-visible low-memory data layout for a future 8086
   5++0669              ; real-mode BIOS. This stage does not implement executable BIOS ROM or INT
   6++0669              ; services. IVT is initialized to zero; vectors are installed only after the
   7++0669              ; CPU INT/IRET path and guest stack semantics are implemented.
   8++0669              ;
   9++0669              ; Physical memory profile:
  10++0669              ;   00000-003FF  IVT (256 entries, 4 bytes each)
  11++0669              ;   00400-004FF  BIOS Data Area (BDA)
  12++0669              ;   00500-9FFFF  conventional RAM profile (640 KiB)
  13++0669              ;   A0000-BFFFF  reserved video memory
  14++0669              ;   C0000-EFFFF  reserved option-ROM/device window
  15++0669              ;   F0000-FFFFF  reserved P124 BIOS ROM (not read-only yet)
  16++0669              ;
  17++0669              ; Entry: bios_post
  18++0669              ; Success: x86_exit_status=0, saved_x86_ax=#B105
  19++0669              ; Failure: x86_exit_status=7, saved_x86_ax=#B1xx
  20++0669              ; No DMA/IPC transport changes.
  21++0669              ; =========================================================================
  22++0669
  23++0669              BIOS_IVT_BASE             EQU #00000
  24++0669              BIOS_IVT_SIZE             EQU #00400
  25++0669              BIOS_BDA_BASE             EQU #00400
  26++0669              BIOS_BDA_SIZE             EQU #00100
  27++0669              BIOS_LOW_MEMORY_END       EQU #00500
  28++0669              BIOS_CONVENTIONAL_KB      EQU 640
  29++0669              BIOS_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT      EQU #03
  30++0669              BIOS_TEXT_COLUMNS         EQU 80
  31++0669              BIOS_TEXT_ROWS            EQU 25
  32++0669              BIOS_POST_SIGNATURE       EQU #B105

   1++08B5              ; =========================================================================
   2++08B5              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 16-bit ModR/M effective-address decoder foundation
   3++08B5              ;
   4++08B5              ; IN:  A = ModR/M byte, BC = pointer to next instruction byte.
   5++08B5              ; OUT: x86_ea_is_reg, x86_ea_reg, x86_ea_offset, x86_ea_segment.
   6++08B5              ;      BC advances past disp8/disp16. Carry set on truncated displacement.
   7++08B5              ;
   8++08B5              ; Register encoding for r/m and reg fields:
   9++08B5              ;   0 AX, 1 CX, 2 DX, 3 BX, 4 SP, 5 BP, 6 SI, 7 DI.
  10++08B5              ; Memory default segment is DS; forms containing BP use SS.
  11++08B5              ; mod=00,r/m=110 is direct [disp16] and uses DS.
  12++08B5              ; =========================================================================
  13++08B5
  14++08B5              x86_decode_modrm16:

  18++08BB 07                   rlca

  20++08BF

  23++08C4 CB 3F                srl     a
  24++08C6 CB 3F                srl     a
  25++08C8 CB 3F                srl     a

   1++0A17              ; =========================================================================
   2++0A17              ; PROJECT 124 — minimal x86 interpreter core for NeoGS Z80
   3++0A17              ;
   4++0A17              ; Supported opcodes:
   5++0A17              ;   90       NOP
   6++0A17              ;   40       INC AX (8086 legacy one-byte register encoding)
   7++0A17              ;   B8 iw    MOV AX, imm16
   8++0A17              ;   F4       HLT (successful task termination)
   9++0A17              ;
  10++0A17              ; Unsupported opcode: status 2. Instruction budget exhausted: status 1.
  11++0A17              ; DMA error: status 3. Out-of-range/truncated instruction: status 4.
  12++0A17              ; x86_program_end is the exclusive logical address after the payload.
  13++0A17              ;
  14++0A17              ; Entry: BC = x86 IP in NeoGS Z80 logical address space; AX stored in
  15++0A17              ;        saved_z80_ax. Exit: saved_z80_ax and x86_exit_status are updated.
  16++0A17              ; =========================================================================
  17++0A17
  18++0A17              x86_execute_loop:
  19++0A17              x86_next_instruction:
  20++0A17                      ; Every opcode fetch must remain strictly below the exclusive end.
  21++0A17 2A 02 0D             ld      hl, (x86_program_end)
  22++0A1A B7                   or      a
  23++0A1B ED 42                sbc     hl, bc
  24++0A1D DA 8A 0A             jp      c, x86_ip_out_of_range
  25++0A20 7C                   ld      a, h
  26++0A21 B5                   or      l
  27++0A22 CA 8A 0A             jp      z, x86_ip_out_of_range
  28++0A25
  29++0A25 2A 00 0D             ld      hl, (x86_steps_remaining)
  30++0A28 7C                   ld      a, h
  31++0A29 B5                   or      l
  32++0A2A 20 09                jr      nz, .budget_ok
  33++0A2C 3E 01                ld      a, 1
  34++0A2E 32 04 0D             ld      (x86_exit_status), a
  35++0A31 2A FC 03             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
  36++0A34 C9                   ret
  37++0A35
  38++0A35              .budget_ok:
  39++0A35 2B                   dec     hl
  40++0A36 22 00 0D             ld      (x86_steps_remaining), hl
  41++0A39
  42++0A39                      ; Fetch opcode and advance IP.
  43++0A39 0A                   ld      a, (bc)
  44++0A3A 03                   inc     bc
  45++0A3B
  46++0A3B                      ; Resolve handler from 256-entry WORD table.
  47++0A3B 6F                   ld      l, a
  48++0A3C 26 00                ld      h, 0
  49++0A3E 29                   add     hl, hl
  50++0A3F 11 00 0B             ld      de, x86_jump_table
  51++0A42 19                   add     hl, de
  52++0A43 5E                   ld      e, (hl)
  53++0A44 23                   inc     hl
  54++0A45 56                   ld      d, (hl)
  55++0A46
  56++0A46                      ; Restore AX in HL and jump to the selected handler. PUSH/RET is
  57++0A46                      ; the Z80's safe indirect-jump idiom; BC remains the virtual x86 IP.
  58++0A46 2A FC 03             ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
  59++0A49 D5                   push    de
  60++0A4A C9                   ret
  61++0A4B
  62++0A4B              ; 90 — NOP
  63++0A4B              x86_nop:

  65++0A4E C3 17 0A             jp      x86_next_instruction
  66++0A51

  68++0A51              x86_inc_ax:

  70++0A52 22 FC 03             ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
  71++0A55 C3 17 0A             jp      x86_next_instruction
  72++0A58

  74++0A58              x86_mov_ax_imm16:

  76++0A58 2A 02 0D             ld      hl, (x86_program_end)

  79++0A5E 11 02 00             ld      de, 2

   1++0D05              ; =========================================================================
   2++0D05              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode interrupt frame engine (Stage 2A)
   3++0D05              ;
   4++0D05              ; Implements the architectural memory/stack part of software INT and IRET.
   5++0D05              ; This module is deliberately separate from the legacy four-opcode fetch
   6++0D05              ; loop: opcode dispatch and execution of guest handler code are Stage 2B.
   7++0D05              ;
   8++0D05              ; x86_int_enter:
   9++0D05              ;   IN: A = interrupt vector number
  10++0D05              ;   Uses x86_reg_{ss,sp,cs,ip,flags}.
  11++0D05              ;   Push order: FLAGS, CS, IP; final SP points to saved IP.
  12++0D05              ;   Loads new IP:CS from IVT[vector], clears IF and TF.
  13++0D05              ;   OUT: carry clear on success, carry set on memory error.
  14++0D05              ;
  15++0D05              ; x86_iret_return:
  16++0D05              ;   Pops IP, CS, FLAGS; restores all three and advances SP by six.
  17++0D05              ;
  18++0D05              ; x86_int_self_test:
  19++0D05              ;   Installs a temporary vector 20h, validates the exact stack frame,
  20++0D05              ;   validates IF/TF clearing, executes IRET, validates restored state,
  21++0D05              ;   and removes the temporary vector.
  22++0D05              ;   Success: status=0, AX=1A01h. Failure: status=8, AX=1Axxh.
  23++0D05              ; =========================================================================
  24++0D05
  25++0D05              X86_INT_IF_TF_CLEAR_MASK EQU #FC
  26++0D05
  27++0D05              x86_int_enter:
  28++0D05 32 22 0F             ld      (x86_int_vector), a
  29++0D08
  30++0D08                      ; IVT entry address = vector * 4, physical segment zero.
  31++0D08 6F                   ld      l, a
  32++0D09 26 00                ld      h, 0
  33++0D0B 29                   add     hl, hl
  34++0D0C 29                   add     hl, hl
  35++0D0D 22 61 06             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
  36++0D10 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  37++0D13 22 5F 06             ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
  38++0D16 CD 24 05             call    x86_mem_read16
  39++0D19 D8                   ret     c
  40++0D1A 22 25 0F             ld      (x86_int_target_ip), hl
  41++0D1D
  42++0D1D 2A 61 06             ld      hl, (x86_mem_offset)
  43++0D20 23                   inc     hl
  44++0D21 23                   inc     hl
  45++0D22 22 61 06             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
  46++0D25 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  47++0D28 22 5F 06             ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
  48++0D2B CD 24 05             call    x86_mem_read16
  49++0D2E D8                   ret     c
  50++0D2F 22 27 0F             ld      (x86_int_target_cs), hl
  51++0D32
  52++0D32                      ; 8086 interrupt entry pushes FLAGS, then CS, then return IP.
  53++0D32 ED 5B 0B 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_flags)
  54++0D36 CD 77 0D             call    x86_int_push_word
  55++0D39 D8                   ret     c
  56++0D3A ED 5B 03 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_cs)
  57++0D3E CD 77 0D             call    x86_int_push_word
  58++0D41 D8                   ret     c
  59++0D42 ED 5B 09 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_ip)
  60++0D46 CD 77 0D             call    x86_int_push_word
  61++0D49 D8                   ret     c
  62++0D4A
  63++0D4A                      ; IF (bit 9) and TF (bit 8) are cleared in the live FLAGS register.
  64++0D4A 3A 0C 0A             ld      a, (x86_reg_flags+1)
  65++0D4D E6 FC                and     X86_INT_IF_TF_CLEAR_MASK
  66++0D4F 32 0C 0A             ld      (x86_reg_flags+1), a
  67++0D52 2A 25 0F             ld      hl, (x86_int_target_ip)
  68++0D55 22 09 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
  69++0D58 2A 27 0F             ld      hl, (x86_int_target_cs)
  70++0D5B 22 03 0A             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
  71++0D5E B7                   or      a
  72++0D5F C9                   ret
  73++0D60
  74++0D60              x86_iret_return:
  75++0D60 CD 97 0D             call    x86_int_pop_word
  76++0D63 D8                   ret     c
  77++0D64 22 09 0A             ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
  78++0D67
  79++0D67 CD 97 0D             call    x86_int_pop_word
  80++0D6A D8                   ret     c
  81++0D6B 22 03 0A             ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
  82++0D6E
  83++0D6E CD 97 0D             call    x86_int_pop_word
  84++0D71 D8                   ret     c
  85++0D72 22 0B 0A             ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl
  86++0D75 B7                   or      a
  87++0D76 C9                   ret
  88++0D77
  89++0D77              ; IN: DE=word. Decrement guest SP by two, then write SS:SP.
  90++0D77              x86_int_push_word:
  91++0D77 ED 53 23 0F          ld      (x86_int_word), de
  92++0D7B 2A F9 09             ld      hl, (x86_reg_sp)
  93++0D7E 2B                   dec     hl
  94++0D7F 2B                   dec     hl
  95++0D80 22 F9 09             ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
  96++0D83 22 61 06             ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
  97++0D86 ED 5B 05 0A          ld      de, (x86_reg_ss)
  98++0D8A ED 53 5F 06          ld      (x86_mem_segment), de
  99++0D8E 2A 23 0F             ld      hl, (x86_int_word)
 100++0D91 22 65 06             ld      (x86_mem_word_value), hl
 101++0D94 C3 44 05             jp      x86_mem_write16
 102++0D97

 104++0D97              x86_int_pop_word:

   1++0F29              ; =========================================================================
   2++0F29              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode fetch/execute core, Stage 2B
   3++0F29              ;
   4++0F29              ; This bounded executable slice runs guest instructions through CS:IP and
   5++0F29              ; the physical-memory backend. Supported: NOP, register-direct 8/16-bit
   6++0F29              ; arithmetic and logical subsets, PUSHF, INC/DEC r16, PUSH/POP r16,
   7++0F29              ; near/far CALL and RET, far JMP, MOV r8/r16,imm, MOV r/m8/r/m16 register-memory forms,
   8++0F29              ; short Jcc, LOOP/JCXZ, JMP rel8, HLT, INT imm8, INT3, IRET.
   9++0F29              ; Arithmetic and 8/16-bit logical memory ModR/M operands support segment overrides.
  10++0F29              ; It is intentionally not a general 8086 emulator.
  11++0F29              ;
  12++0F29              ; x86_real_mode_test installs a guest program at 0000:5002 and an interrupt
  13++0F29              ; handler at 0000:5100. The guest loads all eight general registers with
  14++0F29              ; MOV r16,imm16, invokes INT 20h, then resumes at HLT after handler IRET.
  15++0F29              ; The test checks all MOV destinations, interrupt return, FLAGS and SP.
  16++0F29              ; Success: status=0, AX=1A02h. Failures: status=9/11 with stage-specific AX codes.
  17++0F29              ; =========================================================================
  18++0F29

 450++1296
 451++1296              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 452++1296              ; 50-57 PUSH r16 and 58-5F POP r16. Stack accesses use SS:SP through the
 453++1296              ; physical-memory API. PUSH SP stores the pre-decrement SP value, matching
 454++1296              ; the original 8086 behavior. POP SP increments internally, then replaces
 455++1296              ; SP with the popped word. Neither instruction modifies guest FLAGS.
 456++1296              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------

 638++13F4              ; Short relative control flow: 70-7F Jcc, E0-E3 LOOP/JCXZ, EB JMP.

 640++13F4              ; byte has been fetched. All target fetches still pass through the bounded

 883++15D8
 884++15D8              ; 40-47 INC r16 and 48-4F DEC r16.
 885++15D8              ; The register file is contiguous in 8086 encoding order (AX,CX,DX,BX,
 886++15D8              ; SP,BP,SI,DI). INC/DEC preserve CF and all non-status/control FLAGS bits.
 887++15D8              x86_real_inc_r16:

 889++15DB AF                   xor     a
 890++15DC 32 B1 17             ld      (x86_real_incdec_mode), a

 892++15E2

 895++15E5 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 896++15E7 32 B1 17             ld      (x86_real_incdec_mode), a

 900++15ED E6 07                and     7

 911++15FE 23                   inc     hl
 912++15FF 7E                   ld      a, (hl)

 922++1611 23                   inc     hl
 923++1612 18 01                jr      .store_word
 924++1614              .decrement_word:

 926++1615              .store_word:

 930++161D 12                   ld      (de), a

 933++1620 12                   ld      (de), a

 936++1624 C3 04 11             jp      x86_real_next
 937++1627
 938++1627              ; Update only OF/SF/ZF/AF/PF. CF is copied from the old FLAGS word;
 939++1627              ; all reserved/control bits are retained. PF is even parity of result AL.
 940++1627              x86_real_update_incdec_flags:
 941++1627 3A 0B 0A             ld      a, (x86_reg_flags)
 942++162A E6 01                and     1

 944++162F AF                   xor     a
 945++1630 32 B5 17             ld      (x86_real_new_flags+1), a
 946++1633
 947++1633                      ; PF: even parity of the low result byte.
 948++1633 2A B8 17             ld      hl, (x86_real_result)
 949++1636 7D                   ld      a, l
 950++1637 A7                   and     a
 951++1638 EA 3D 16             jp      pe, .set_pf
 952++163B 18 05                jr      .no_pf
 953++163D              .set_pf:
 954++163D 21 B4 17             ld      hl, x86_real_new_flags
 955++1640 CB D6                set     2, (hl)
 956++1642              .no_pf:
 957++1642                      ; ZF: full 16-bit result is zero.
 958++1642 2A B8 17             ld      hl, (x86_real_result)
 959++1645 7C                   ld      a, h
 960++1646 B5                   or      l
 961++1647 20 05                jr      nz, .no_zf
 962++1649 21 B4 17             ld      hl, x86_real_new_flags
 963++164C CB F6                set     6, (hl)
 964++164E              .no_zf:

 970++1658 CB FE                set     7, (hl)

 975++165F 47                   ld      b, a

   1++1859              ; =========================================================================
   2++1859              ; PROJECT 124 — 8086 real-mode 16-bit ALU (register and memory ModR/M)
   3++1859              ;
   4++1859              ; Supported 16-bit ModR/M forms:
   5++1859              ; ADD 01/03, ADC 11/13, SBB 19/1B, SUB 29/2B, CMP 39/3B.
   6++1859              ; Register and memory operands are supported in both directions. Memory
   7++1859              ; effective addresses and segment overrides use the shared MOV decoder.
   8++1859              ; 8-bit arithmetic and logical memory forms remain unsupported.
   9++1859              ; PUSHF 9C is included to make guest FLAGS assertions observable.
  10++1859              ; =========================================================================

 616++1CF0              ; Guest at 0800:5800:

 618++1CF0              x86_alu_memory_guest_image:
 619++1CF0 01 07                db #01,#07                 ; ADD DS:[BX],AX: 5 + 3 = 8
 620++1CF2 03 0F                db #03,#0F                 ; ADD CX,DS:[BX]: 2 + 8 = 10

 622++1CF7 2B D1                db #2B,#D1                 ; SUB DX,CX: set CF

 627++1D03 29 46 00             db #29,#46,#00             ; SUB SS:[BP],AX: 1C - 3 = 19

 629++1D08 BA 00 00             db #BA,#00,#00             ; MOV DX,0
 630++1D0B 2B D1                db #2B,#D1                 ; SUB DX,CX: set CF for ADC
 631++1D0D 13 0F                db #13,#0F                 ; ADC CX,DS:[BX]: 10 + 12 + 1 = 23
 632++1D0F 2B 0F                db #2B,#0F                 ; SUB CX,DS:[BX]: 23 - 12 = 11

 896++2F7D              ;         pop     af
 897++2F7D              ;         ret
 898++2F7D              ;
 899++2F7D              ; x86_mov_trace_putc:
 900++2F7D              ;         ; NeoGS local I/O is not the Spectrum printer port. Send each
 901++2F7D              ;         ; trace byte through the existing DBIT stream; the host drains it
 902++2F7D              ;         ; while CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST is active and mirrors it to port #EF.
 903++2F7D              ;         push    bc
 904++2F7D              ;         push    de
 905++2F7D              ;         push    hl
 906++2F7D              ;         call    neo_send_data_byte
 907++2F7D              ;         pop     hl
 908++2F7D              ;         pop     de
 909++2F7D              ;         pop     bc
 910++2F7D              ;         ret
 911++2F7D              ;
 912++2F7D              ; ; IN A=byte.
 913++2F7D              ; x86_mov_trace_hex8:
 914++2F7D              ;         push    af
 915++2F7D              ;         rra
 916++2F7D              ;         rra
 917++2F7D              ;         rra
 918++2F7D              ;         rra
 919++2F7D              ;         call    x86_mov_trace_nibble
 920++2F7D              ;         pop     af
 921++2F7D              ;         jp      x86_mov_trace_nibble
 922++2F7D              ;
 923++2F7D              ; x86_mov_trace_nibble:
 924++2F7D              ;         and     #0F
 925++2F7D              ;         cp      10
 926++2F7D              ;         jr      c, .trace_digit
 927++2F7D              ;         add     a, 7
 928++2F7D              ; .trace_digit:
 929++2F7D              ;         add     a, '0'
 930++2F7D              ;         jp      x86_mov_trace_putc
 931++2F7D              ;
 932++2F7D              ; ; IN HL=word.
 933++2F7D              ; x86_mov_trace_hex16:
 934++2F7D              ;         ld      a, h
 935++2F7D              ;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex8
 936++2F7D              ;         ld      a, l
 937++2F7D              ;         jp      x86_mov_trace_hex8
 938++2F7D              ;
 939++2F7D              ;

   1++2F7D              ; =========================================================================
   2++2F7D              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS INT 10h text services, Stage 3A
   3++2F7D              ;
   4++2F7D              ; Project text-console profile: 42 columns x 12 rows, 6x8 glyphs.
   5++2F7D              ; Implemented functions:
   6++2F7D              ;   AH=00h set mode 03h (project 42x12 text profile)
   7++2F7D              ;   AH=0Fh get mode (AL=03h, AH=42, BH=0)
   8++2F7D              ;   AH=02h set cursor (BH=0, DH=row, DL=column)
   9++2F7D              ;   AH=03h get cursor (DH=row, DL=column)
  10++2F7D              ;   AH=0Eh teletype output (AL=character, BH=0)
  11++2F7D              ;
  12++2F7D              ; INT 10h enters through the normal IVT/stack frame. The BIOS service itself
  13++2F7D              ; is a firmware trap, then returns through the normal IRET primitive.
  14++2F7D              ; Host stream protocol over DBIT/GSDAT:
  15++2F7D              ;   nonzero byte = teletype character
  16++2F7D              ;   00 00        = clear the lower debug console
  17++2F7D              ;   00 01 row col= set lower debug console cursor
  18++2F7D              ; =========================================================================
  19++2F7D
  20++2F7D              BIOS_VIDEO_COLUMNS EQU 42
  21++2F7D              BIOS_VIDEO_ROWS    EQU 12
  22++2F7D              BIOS_VIDEO_MODE    EQU #03

  29++2F86 FE 0F                cp      #0F

  38++2F9B C9                   ret

  11  33BB              ; The first firmware segment occupies the full low window $0000-$3FFF.

  17  33BB              ; GS105a #14 writes the executable bootstrap into fixed RAM $4000-$7FFF.
  18  33BB                      ORG     $4000
  19  4000                      INCLUDE "bootstrap.asm"
# file opened: bootstrap.asm
   1+ 4000              ; =========================================================================
   2+ 4000              ; PROJECT 124 — first-stage NeoGS executable bootstrap
   3+ 4000              ;
   4+ 4000              ; GS105a command #14 (RECEIVE BLOCK) loads this raw Z80 image into fixed RAM
   5+ 4000              ; at $4000. GS105a command #13 (JUMP TO ADDRESS) enters it at $4000.
   6+ 4000              ;
   7+ 4000              ; The bootstrap maps RAM over the low ROM window, receives a 16-bit firmware
   8+ 4000              ; length and exactly that many bytes through the documented ZX data channel,
   9+ 4000              ; stores the custom firmware at RAM address $0000, then jumps to $0000.
  10+ 4000              ;
  11+ 4000              ; It executes from fixed RAM window $4000-$7FFF. Its stack is moved to
  12+ 4000              ; reserved $F000-$FFFF after the extended RAM windows are configured.
  13+ 4000              ; =========================================================================
  14+ 4000
  15+ 4000              neogs_bootstrap_start:
  16+ 4000 F3                   di
  17+ 4001 31 FF 3F             ld      sp, #3FFF
  18+ 4004
  19+ 4004                      ; Enable writable RAM-over-ROM and independent WIN2/WIN3 paging.
  20+ 4004 DB 0F                in      a, (GSCFG0)
  21+ 4006 E6 FD                and     #FD                    ; clear RAMRO: all target RAM must be writable
  22+ 4008 F6 01                or      M_NOROM                ; map RAM over the stock ROM at $0000
  23+ 400A F6 08                or      M_EXPAG                ; MPAG and MPAGEX independently select WIN2/WIN3
  24+ 400C D3 0F                out     (GSCFG0), a
  25+ 400E
  26+ 400E                      ; Physical 16-KiB page map used by the 48-KiB firmware layout:
  27+ 400E                      ; WIN0 $0000-$3FFF = fixed RAM page 0
  28+ 400E                      ; WIN1 $4000-$7FFF = fixed RAM page 3 (includes untouched DMA $5000-$7FFF)
  29+ 400E                      ; WIN2 $8000-$BFFF = RAM page 1; ROL8 encoding of page 1 is $80
  30+ 400E                      ; WIN3 $C000-$FFFF = RAM page 2; ROL8 encoding of page 2 is $01
  31+ 400E 3E 80                ld      a, #80
  32+ 4010 D3 00                out     (MPAG), a
  33+ 4012 3E 01                ld      a, #01
  34+ 4014 D3 10                out     (MPAGEX), a
  35+ 4016
  36+ 4016                      ; Stack lives in the reserved $F000-$FFFF part of WIN3. This keeps it
  37+ 4016                      ; safe while the bootstrap fills the low and upper firmware windows.
  38+ 4016 31 FE FF             ld      sp, #FFFE
  39+ 4019
  40+ 4019                      ; Tell Spectrum that the tiny loader is running and waiting for length.
  41+ 4019 3E A1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
  42+ 401B CD 98 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  43+ 401E
  44+ 401E                      ; Host sends length low byte, then high byte.
  45+ 401E CD 84 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  46+ 4021 5F                   ld      e, a
  47+ 4022 CD 84 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  48+ 4025 57                   ld      d, a
  49+ 4026
  50+ 4026                      ; Reject zero length and any image larger than the low 16-KiB window ($4000).
  51+ 4026 7A                   ld      a, d
  52+ 4027 B3                   or      e
  53+ 4028 28 22                jr      z, bootstrap_error
  54+ 402A 7A                   ld      a, d
  55+ 402B FE 40                cp      #40
  56+ 402D 38 06                jr      c, bootstrap_length_ok
  57+ 402F 20 1B                jr      nz, bootstrap_error
  58+ 4031 7B                   ld      a, e
  59+ 4032 B7                   or      a
  60+ 4033 20 17                jr      nz, bootstrap_error
  61+ 4035
  62+ 4035              bootstrap_length_ok:
  63+ 4035                      ; Explicitly acknowledge that the length is accepted before the host
  64+ 4035                      ; starts streaming the firmware payload.
  65+ 4035 3E A3                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
  66+ 4037 CD 98 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  67+ 403A 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
  68+ 403D
  69+ 403D              bootstrap_copy_loop:
  70+ 403D 7A                   ld      a, d
  71+ 403E B3                   or      e
  72+ 403F 28 08                jr      z, bootstrap_start_firmware
  73+ 4041 CD 84 40             call    bootstrap_receive_data_byte
  74+ 4044 77                   ld      (hl), a
  75+ 4045 23                   inc     hl
  76+ 4046 1B                   dec     de
  77+ 4047 18 F4                jr      bootstrap_copy_loop
  78+ 4049
  79+ 4049              bootstrap_start_firmware:
  80+ 4049 C3 00 00             jp      0
  81+ 404C
  82+ 404C              bootstrap_error:
  83+ 404C 3E E1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK
  84+ 404E CD 98 40             call    bootstrap_send_data_byte
  85+ 4051 C3 54 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
  86+ 4054
  87+ 4054              ; Terminal state used when the host stops responding or cannot consume data.
  88+ 4054              ; Never leave a bootstrap polling loop running forever with no timeout.
  89+ 4054              bootstrap_halt:
  90+ 4054 F3                   di
  91+ 4055              .bootstrap_halt_loop:
  92+ 4055 76                   halt
  93+ 4056 18 FD                jr      .bootstrap_halt_loop
  94+ 4058
  95+ 4058              ; Best-effort error ACK after a receive timeout. This path has its own
  96+ 4058              ; bounded waits and never calls bootstrap_send_data_byte recursively.
  97+ 4058              bootstrap_timeout:
  98+ 4058 3E E1                ld      a, NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK
  99+ 405A 32 C3 40             ld      (bootstrap_tx_byte), a
 100+ 405D 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 101+ 4060              .timeout_wait_slot:
 102+ 4060 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 103+ 4062 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 104+ 4064 28 08                jr      z, .timeout_slot_ready
 105+ 4066 0B                   dec     bc
 106+ 4067 78                   ld      a, b
 107+ 4068 B1                   or      c
 108+ 4069 20 F5                jr      nz, .timeout_wait_slot
 109+ 406B C3 54 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
 110+ 406E              .timeout_slot_ready:
 111+ 406E 3A C3 40             ld      a, (bootstrap_tx_byte)
 112+ 4071 D3 03                out     (ZXDATWR), a
 113+ 4073 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 114+ 4076              .timeout_wait_host:
 115+ 4076 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 116+ 4078 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 117+ 407A 28 D8                jr      z, bootstrap_halt
 118+ 407C 0B                   dec     bc
 119+ 407D 78                   ld      a, b
 120+ 407E B1                   or      c
 121+ 407F 20 F5                jr      nz, .timeout_wait_host
 122+ 4081 C3 54 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
 123+ 4084
 124+ 4084              ; Wait for one byte from Spectrum. Reading ZXDATRD clears DBIT.
 125+ 4084              bootstrap_receive_data_byte:
 126+ 4084 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 127+ 4087              .rx_wait:
 128+ 4087 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 129+ 4089 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 130+ 408B 20 08                jr      nz, .rx_ready
 131+ 408D 0B                   dec     bc
 132+ 408E 78                   ld      a, b
 133+ 408F B1                   or      c
 134+ 4090 20 F5                jr      nz, .rx_wait
 135+ 4092 C3 58 40             jp      bootstrap_timeout
 136+ 4095              .rx_ready:
 137+ 4095 DB 02                in      a, (ZXDATRD)
 138+ 4097 C9                   ret
 139+ 4098
 140+ 4098              ; Send one byte to Spectrum. Host reads GSDAT and clears DBIT.
 141+ 4098              bootstrap_send_data_byte:
 142+ 4098 32 C3 40             ld      (bootstrap_tx_byte), a
 143+ 409B 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 144+ 409E              .tx_free:
 145+ 409E DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 146+ 40A0 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 147+ 40A2 28 08                jr      z, .tx_slot_free
 148+ 40A4 0B                   dec     bc
 149+ 40A5 78                   ld      a, b
 150+ 40A6 B1                   or      c
 151+ 40A7 20 F5                jr      nz, .tx_free
 152+ 40A9 C3 54 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
 153+ 40AC              .tx_slot_free:
 154+ 40AC 3A C3 40             ld      a, (bootstrap_tx_byte)
 155+ 40AF D3 03                out     (ZXDATWR), a
 156+ 40B1 01 FF FF             ld      bc, #FFFF
 157+ 40B4              .tx_wait_read:
 158+ 40B4 DB 04                in      a, (ZXSTAT)
 159+ 40B6 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
 160+ 40B8 28 08                jr      z, .tx_done
 161+ 40BA 0B                   dec     bc
 162+ 40BB 78                   ld      a, b
 163+ 40BC B1                   or      c
 164+ 40BD 20 F5                jr      nz, .tx_wait_read
 165+ 40BF C3 54 40             jp      bootstrap_halt
 166+ 40C2              .tx_done:
 167+ 40C2 C9                   ret
 168+ 40C3
 169+ 40C3              bootstrap_tx_byte:
 170+ 40C3 00                   db      0
 171+ 40C4
 172+ 40C4              neogs_bootstrap_end:
 173+ 40C4
# file closed: bootstrap.asm
  20  40C4                      ASSERT neogs_bootstrap_start = $4000
  21  40C4                      ASSERT neogs_bootstrap_end <= $7FFF
  22  40C4              ; The host keeps the first TR-DOS sector in its reusable #C000 buffer while
  23  40C4              ; it installs and starts this bootstrap. Header + bootstrap must fit in 256 bytes.
  24  40C4                      ASSERT neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start <= 254
  25  40C4                      SAVEBIN "neogs_bootstrap.bin", neogs_bootstrap_start, neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start
  26  40C4                      SAVEBIN "zx_main/neogs_bootstrap.bin", neogs_bootstrap_start, neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start
